    def extract_audio_stream(
        self,
        url_or_path: str,
        output_wav_path: Optional[str] = None,
        job_id: Optional[str] = None,
        output_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Path:
        path = Path(url_or_path)
    
        # Contract 1: Local WAV return
        if path.exists() and path.suffix.lower() in [".wav", ".mp3"] and not output_wav_path and not output_dir:
            return path
    
        # Determine target directory and filename
        if output_wav_path:
            out_path = Path(output_wav_path)
            target_dir = out_path.parent
        elif output_dir:
            target_dir = Path(output_dir)
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
        else:
            target_dir = Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / (job_id or "default")
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
    
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
        # Contract 2: Local video file conversion
        if path.exists():
            if path.suffix.lower() == out_path.suffix.lower():
                if path != out_path:
                    shutil.copy(path, out_path)
                return out_path
    
            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-i",
                str(path),
                "-vn",
                "-acodec",
                "libmp3lame" if out_path.suffix.lower() == ".mp3" else "pcm_s16le",
                "-ar",
                "16000",
                "-ac",
                "1",
                "-b:a",
                "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            return out_path
    
        # Contract 3: Remote stream extraction via FFmpeg
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-ss", "0",
            "-i", url_or_path,
            "-vn",
            "-acodec", "libmp3lame",
            "-ar", "16000",
            "-ac", "1",
            "-b:a", "32k",
            str(out_path),
        ]
    
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=180)
            if res.returncode == 0 and out_path.exists() and out_path.stat().st_size > 0:
                return out_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("FFmpeg direct compressed audio extraction failed: %s", exc)
    
        # Fallback via yt-dlp
        ydl_opts = {
            "format": "bestaudio/best",
            "outtmpl": str(out_path).replace(out_path.suffix, ""),
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "postprocessors": [
                {
                    "key": "FFmpegExtractAudio",
                    "preferredcodec": "mp3" if out_path.suffix.lower() == ".mp3" else "wav",
                    "preferredquality": "32",
                }
            ],
            "postprocessor_args": ["-ar", "16000", "-ac", "1"],
        }
    
        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                ydl.download([url_or_path])
    
            if out_path.exists() and out_path.stat().st_size > 0:
                return out_path
    
            # Check for alternative generated audio files in directory
            candidates = list(target_dir.glob(f"*{job_id or ''}*"))
            for cand in candidates:
                if cand.suffix.lower() in [".wav", ".mp3"] and cand.stat().st_size > 0:
                    return cand
        except Exception as exc:
            raise IngestionError(f"Audio extraction failed for {url_or_path}: {exc}") from exc

124. Nothing's necessarily broken yet — 12 seconds between your POST and GET is likely just not enough time. Here's why I wouldn't call this a failure based on what you've shown:

**`get_yt_dlp_options()`** **sets** **`retries: 5`** **but no explicit socket timeout.** If `ok.ru` is actually resetting the SSL handshake (which is exactly what your own test table predicted for this row), yt-dlp will retry that failing connection up to 5 times, and each attempt can take many seconds to time out at the OS/network level before yt-dlp gives up and moves to the next retry. Five retries at even 10-20s each is well past your 12-second polling window. `"status": "processing"` with everything still `null` at this point is consistent with "still retrying," not "silently broken" — and we already confirmed `_process_job_task` correctly catches exceptions and writes `FAILED` + `error_message` once it actually finishes, so a genuine failure wouldn't stay stuck at `"processing"` forever.

**Two things to actually do, not just wait blindly:**

1. **Check your uvicorn terminal window right now** — not the API response, the actual console where you ran `uvicorn`. `logger.warning`/`logger.error` calls in `ingestion.py` and `pipeline.py` print there, not into the JSON response. If it's grinding through retries or already hit `_parse_friendly_error`, you'll see it there before the job record updates.
2. **Poll** **`GET /api/v1/jobs/job_89c0e33db2`** **again after another minute or two.** If it's still `"processing"` after \~2-3 minutes total, *that's* the point where I'd call it a real hang rather than "still retrying," and we'd look at adding an explicit timeout.

**Better than waiting on the async flow at all** — run the isolated script I gave you last turn (`quick_test_okru.py`, calling `probe_metadata` directly, no FastAPI, no background task). That gives you a synchronous answer in one terminal, no polling ambiguity, and tells us definitively whether this is a real network condition or something in the async plumbing. Did you get a chance to run that one, or did you go straight to the API?

import json
import logging
import math
import shutil
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, List, Optional

import cv2

from src.config import settings
from src.fallback_vlm import VLMArbiterService, VLMError
from src.ingestion import IngestionError, StreamIngestionService
from src.models.schemas import (
    CandidateFrame,
    DetectionResult,
    JobRequest,
    JobStatus,
    STTResult,
    TierType,
)
from src.primary_ocr import (
    OCRError,
    scan_dense_window,
    scan_sparse_timeline,
)
from src.primary_stt import SpeechToTextService


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def format_timestamp(seconds: float) -> str:
    if not math.isfinite(seconds) or seconds < 0:
        seconds = 0.0

    hrs = int(seconds // 3600)
    mins = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)
    secs = seconds % 60

    return f"{hrs:02d}:{mins:02d}:{secs:06.3f}"


def calculate_confidence_score(
    text_similarity: float,
    ocr_confidence: float = 0.0,
    alignment_score: float = 0.0,
    w_sim: float = 0.5,
    w_ocr: float = 0.3,
    w_align: float = 0.2,
) -> float:

    score = (
        w_sim * text_similarity
        + w_ocr * ocr_confidence
        + w_align * alignment_score
    )

    return round(max(0.0, min(1.0, score)), 4)


def _sanitize_path(val: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    if isinstance(val, (str, Path)):
        value = str(val).strip()
        return value if value else None

    return None



class PipelineOrchestrator:

    def __init__(
        self,
        ingestion_service: Optional[StreamIngestionService] = None,
        stt_service: Optional[SpeechToTextService] = None,
        vlm_service: Optional[VLMArbiterService] = None,
        mistral_client: Optional[Any] = None,
    ):

        self.ingestion_service = (
            ingestion_service
            or StreamIngestionService()
        )

        self.stt_service = (
            stt_service
            or SpeechToTextService()
        )

        self.vlm_service = (
            vlm_service
            or VLMArbiterService()
        )

        self.mistral_client = mistral_client



    # ======================================================
    # STREAMING FRAME EXTRACTION
    # ======================================================

    def _extract_single_frame(
        self,
        video_source: str,
        timestamp_seconds: float,
        fps: float,
        dest_path: Path,
    ) -> bool:

        """
        OpenCV fallback.
        Works with:
        - local files
        - direct stream URLs
        """

        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_source)

        if not cap.isOpened():
            logger.warning(
                "Unable to open stream: %s",
                video_source[:100]
            )
            return False


        try:

            frame_number = int(
                round(timestamp_seconds * fps)
            )

            cap.set(
                cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES,
                frame_number
            )


            ret, frame = cap.read()


            if not ret or frame is None:
                return False


            dest_path.parent.mkdir(
                parents=True,
                exist_ok=True
            )

            return bool(
                cv2.imwrite(
                    str(dest_path),
                    frame
                )
            )


        finally:
            cap.release()



    def _persist_frame(
        self,
        artifacts_dir: Path,
        job_id: str,
        tier_label: str,
        video_source: Optional[str],
        timestamp_seconds: float,
        fps: float,
    ) -> Optional[str]:


        if not video_source:
            return None


        artifacts_dir.mkdir(
            parents=True,
            exist_ok=True
        )


        frame_path = (
            artifacts_dir /
            f"{job_id}_{tier_label}.jpg"
        )


        # PRIMARY PATH:
        # Direct stream -> FFmpeg -> Frame
        if self.ingestion_service.extract_frame_on_demand(
            video_source,
            timestamp_seconds,
            frame_path
        ):

            logger.info(
                "Frame extracted from stream at %.2fs",
                timestamp_seconds
            )

            return str(frame_path)



        # FALLBACK:
        # OpenCV stream reader

        if self._extract_single_frame(
            video_source,
            timestamp_seconds,
            fps,
            frame_path
        ):

            return str(frame_path)



        logger.warning(
            "Unable to extract frame at %.2fs",
            timestamp_seconds
        )

        return None




    def _save_job_metadata(
        self,
        artifacts_dir: Path,
        result: DetectionResult
    ):

        try:

            artifacts_dir.mkdir(
                parents=True,
                exist_ok=True
            )

            metadata_file = (
                artifacts_dir /
                "metadata.json"
            )

            metadata_file.write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    result.model_dump(),
                    indent=2
                )
            )

        except Exception as e:

            logger.warning(
                "Metadata save failed: %s",
                e
            )



    # ======================================================
    # MAIN PIPELINE
    # ======================================================


    def run(
        self,
        job_id: str,
        request: JobRequest
    ) -> DetectionResult:


        temp_dir_str = tempfile.mkdtemp(
            prefix=f"quest1_{job_id}_"
        )

        temp_dir = Path(temp_dir_str)


        artifacts_dir = (
            Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir)
            /
            job_id
        )


        logger.info(
            "Executing job %s",
            job_id
        )


        try:


            # ==================================================
            # STREAM PROBE
            # ==================================================

            metadata = (
                self.ingestion_service
                .probe_metadata(
                    request.url
                )
            )


            fps = (
                metadata.fps
                if metadata.fps > 0
                else 25.0
            )


            # IMPORTANT:
            # USE DIRECT STREAM.
            # NEVER DOWNLOAD VIDEO.

            video_source = (
                metadata.stream_path
                or request.url
            )


            if metadata.is_local:

                logger.info(
                    "Processing local video: %s",
                    video_source
                )

            else:

                logger.info(
                    "Processing remote stream: %s",
                    video_source[:120]
                )



            # ==================================================
            # TIER 0 : SUBTITLE MATCH
            # ==================================================

            if metadata.has_subtitles:

                tier0_result = (
                    self.ingestion_service
                    .probe_embedded_subtitles_match(
                        request.url,
                        request.target_text,
                        similarity_threshold=85.0,
                    )
                )


                if (
                    tier0_result
                    and
                    tier0_result.similarity_score >= 0.85
                ):

                    timestamp = (
                        tier0_result.start_time
                    )

                    frame = self._persist_frame(
                        artifacts_dir,
                        job_id,
                        "tier0",
                        video_source,
                        timestamp,
                        fps,
                    )


                    result = DetectionResult(
                        job_id=job_id,
                        status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                        target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                        timestamp_seconds=timestamp,
                        formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(timestamp),
                        frame_number=int(
                            timestamp * fps
                        ),
                        extracted_text=tier0_result.matched_text,
                        confidence_score=tier0_result.similarity_score,
                        tier_executed=TierType.TIER_0_SUBTITLE,
                        frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(frame),
                    )


                    self._save_job_metadata(
                        artifacts_dir,
                        result
                    )


                    return result

                ),

        except IngestionError as e:

            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=f"VLM failed: {e}",
            )


        except Exception as e:

            logger.exception(
                "Pipeline crashed: %s",
                e
            )

            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=str(e),
            )


        finally:

            shutil.rmtree(
                temp_dir_str,
                ignore_errors=True
            )
Also replace your _extract_real_vlm_candidates() with this streaming-safe version:
def _extract_real_vlm_candidates(
    self,
    temp_dir,
    video_source,
    stt_result,
    metadata
):

    candidates = []

    fps = (
        metadata.fps
        if metadata.fps > 0
        else 25.0
    )

    duration = metadata.duration_seconds


    if stt_result.found:

        base = stt_result.start_time

        timestamps = [
            max(0, base - 3),
            max(0, base - 2),
            max(0, base - 1),
            base,
            base + 1,
            base + 2,
            base + 3,
        ]

    else:

        timestamps = [
            duration * i / 8
            for i in range(1,8)
        ]


    for idx, ts in enumerate(
        timestamps,
        start=1
    ):

        frame_path = (
            temp_dir /
            f"candidate_C{idx}.jpg"
        )


        success = (
            self.ingestion_service
            .extract_frame_on_demand(
                video_source,
                ts,
                frame_path
            )
        )


        if success and frame_path.exists():

            candidates.append(
                CandidateFrame(
                    candidate_id=f"C{idx}",
                    timestamp_seconds=round(ts,3),
                    frame_number=int(ts*fps),
                    image_path=str(frame_path)
                )
            )


    return candidates

127. I checked this version. **This is much closer to the streaming architecture we discussed.** You have removed the assumption that the whole video is downloaded and are now passing a `video_source` (stream URL/local path) through the pipeline. The overall direction is correct.

However, this is **not 100% "stream-only" yet**. There are a few places where it still behaves like the old download pipeline.

## ✅ What is correctly changed

### 1. Metadata probing

```python
metadata = self.ingestion_service.probe_metadata(request.url)

video_source = metadata.stream_path or request.url
```

Good. This allows:

```
URL
 |
probe metadata
 |
stream_path
 |
FFmpeg/OpenCV directly reads frames
```

No full download.

---

### 2. Frame extraction

This part is correct:

```python
self.ingestion_service.extract_frame_on_demand(
    video_source,
    timestamp_seconds,
    frame_path
)
```

Your pipeline now does:

```
Remote Video
      |
      |
      v
FFmpeg seek
      |
      |
      v
Single JPEG frame
```

Instead of:

```
Download entire video
        |
        |
        v
Open video file
        |
        |
        v
Extract frame
```

Huge improvement.

---

### 3. VLM candidates

This is also correctly changed:

```python
self.ingestion_service.extract_frame_on_demand(
    video_source,
    ts,
    frame_path
)
```

Your VLM tier now samples directly from the stream.

# Problems remaining

## Problem 1: STT still probably downloads audio

Here:

```python
audio_path = self.ingestion_service.extract_audio_stream(
    video_source,
    job_id=job_id,
    output_dir=temp_dir,
)
```

The name says stream, but we need to verify the implementation.

If internally you have:

```python
ffmpeg -i url output.mp3
```

then you are still downloading the entire audio.

What you want:

```
Stream URL
      |
      |
      v
FFmpeg pipe
      |
      |
      v
Whisper
```

not:

```
Stream URL
      |
      |
      v
download mp3
      |
      |
      v
Whisper
```

Your ingestion.py should have something like:

```python
ffmpeg -i <url> -f wav pipe:1
```

or

```python
subprocess.PIPE
```

---

## Problem 2: OCR scanning functions

These:

```python
scan_sparse_timeline(
    video_source,
    request.target_text,
    self.mistral_client
)
```

and

```python
scan_dense_window(
    video_source,
    start_time,
    end_time,
    fps,
)
```

are only streaming if **inside primary_ocr.py** you changed frame extraction.

If they still do:

```python
cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
```

they are okay for local files but unreliable for HTTP streams.

They should internally use:

```python
ffmpeg -ss timestamp -i stream_url
```

or your:

```python
extract_frame_on_demand()
```

function.

---

## Problem 3: Missing VLM artifact persistence

Old code had:

```python
shutil.copy2(
    selected_cand.image_path,
    persistent_frame_path
)
```

You removed this.

Now:

```python
frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(selected.image_path)
```

means the image lives inside:

```
temp_dir
```

But later you delete:

```python
finally:
    shutil.rmtree(temp_dir_str)
```

So your returned path becomes invalid.

You need to restore:

```python
artifacts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

persistent_path = artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_{selected.candidate_id}.jpg"

shutil.copy2(
    selected.image_path,
    persistent_path
)
```

Then:

```python
frame_image_path=str(persistent_path)
```

Otherwise VLM results will return a broken image path.

---

## Problem 4: This line can crash

```python
video_source[:120]
```

Here:

```python
logger.info(
    "Processing remote stream: %s",
    video_source[:120]
)
```

If:

```python
video_source=None
```

you get:


import difflib
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union

import cv2
import yt_dlp

from src.config import settings
from src.models.schemas import SubtitleMatchResult, VideoMetadata

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class IngestionError(Exception):
    """Domain exception raised when stream ingestion or metadata probing fails."""
    pass


class SubtitleCue:
    def __init__(self, start_seconds: float, end_seconds: float, text: str):
        self.start_seconds = start_seconds
        self.end_seconds = end_seconds
        self.text = text

    def __getitem__(self, item: str) -> Any:
        if item in ("start_seconds", "start_time", "start"):
            return self.start_seconds
        if item in ("end_seconds", "end_time", "end"):
            return self.end_seconds
        if item in ("text", "matched_text"):
            return self.text
        return getattr(self, item)

    def get(self, item: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        try:
            return self[item]
        except (KeyError, AttributeError):
            return default


def _get_cue_val(cue: Union[dict, SubtitleCue, Any], key: str) -> Any:
    if isinstance(cue, dict):
        if key == "start_seconds":
            return cue.get("start_seconds", cue.get("start_time", cue.get("start")))
        if key == "end_seconds":
            return cue.get("end_seconds", cue.get("end_time", cue.get("end")))
        return cue.get(key)
    try:
        return cue[key]
    except Exception:
        return getattr(cue, key, None)


class StreamIngestionService:
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_text(text: str) -> str:
        text = text.lower()
        text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s]", "", text)
        return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()

    normalize_text = _normalize_text

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_vtt_timestamp(ts_str: str) -> float:
        ts_str = str(ts_str).strip().replace(",", ".")
        parts = ts_str.split(":")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return float(parts[0]) * 3600 + float(parts[1]) * 60 + float(parts[2])
        elif len(parts) == 2:
            return float(parts[0]) * 60 + float(parts[1])
        return float(ts_str)

    parse_vtt_timestamp = _parse_vtt_timestamp

    def _resolve_direct_stream_url(self, url_or_path: str) -> str:
        if not url_or_path.startswith("http") or Path(url_or_path).exists():
            return url_or_path

        try:
            meta = self.probe_metadata(url_or_path)
            if meta.stream_path and meta.stream_path.startswith("http"):
                return meta.stream_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Could not resolve direct stream URL for %s: %s", url_or_path, exc)

        return url_or_path

    def _parse_vtt_file(self, file_path: Union[str, Path]) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        path = Path(file_path)
        if not path.exists():
            return []

        cues: List[SubtitleCue] = []
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            lines = f.readlines()

        cue_regex = re.compile(
            r"(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}\s+-->\s+(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}"
        )

        i = 0
        while i < len(lines):
            line = lines[i].strip()
            if "-->" in line and cue_regex.search(line):
                parts = line.split("-->")
                start_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[0].split()[0])
                end_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[1].split()[0])

                text_lines = []
                i += 1
                while i < len(lines) and lines[i].strip() and "-->" not in lines[i]:
                    text_lines.append(lines[i].strip())
                    i += 1

                raw_text = " ".join(text_lines)
                clean_text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", raw_text).strip()
                if clean_text:
                    cues.append(SubtitleCue(start_sec, end_sec, clean_text))
                continue
            i += 1

        return cues

    parse_vtt_file = _parse_vtt_file

    def _match_phrase_in_cues(
        self,
        target_phrase: str,
        cues: List[Union[dict, SubtitleCue]],
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: float = 2.0,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        if not target_phrase or not cues:
            return None

        norm_target = self._normalize_text(target_phrase)
        if not norm_target:
            return None

        target_words = norm_target.split()
        threshold_ratio = similarity_threshold / 100.0 if similarity_threshold > 1.0 else similarity_threshold

        for cue in cues:
            cue_text = _get_cue_val(cue, "text") or ""
            norm_cue = self._normalize_text(cue_text)

            if norm_target in norm_cue:
                return SubtitleMatchResult(
                    start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                    end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                    matched_text=cue_text,
                    similarity_score=1.0,
                    track_language=track_language,
                    is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                )

            if norm_cue:
                score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, norm_cue).ratio()
                if score >= threshold_ratio and len(target_words) <= len(norm_cue.split()) + 2:
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                        end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                        matched_text=cue_text,
                        similarity_score=score,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

        for start_idx in range(len(cues)):
            accumulated_words = []
            accumulated_raw_text = []
            start_time = float(_get_cue_val(cues[start_idx], "start_seconds"))

            for current_idx in range(start_idx, len(cues)):
                curr_cue = cues[current_idx]

                if current_idx > start_idx:
                    prev_cue = cues[current_idx - 1]
                    gap = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "start_seconds")) - float(_get_cue_val(prev_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    if gap > max_gap_seconds:
                        break

                curr_text = _get_cue_val(curr_cue, "text") or ""
                norm_words = self._normalize_text(curr_text).split()
                accumulated_words.extend(norm_words)
                accumulated_raw_text.append(curr_text)

                accumulated_phrase = " ".join(accumulated_words)

                if norm_target in accumulated_phrase:
                    end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=start_time,
                        end_time=end_time,
                        matched_text=full_matched_text,
                        similarity_score=1.0,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

                if accumulated_phrase:
                    score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, accumulated_phrase).ratio()
                    if score >= threshold_ratio and abs(len(accumulated_words) - len(target_words)) <= 3:
                        end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                        full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                        return SubtitleMatchResult(
                            start_time=start_time,
                            end_time=end_time,
                            matched_text=full_matched_text,
                            similarity_score=score,
                            track_language=track_language,
                            is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                        )

                if len(accumulated_words) > len(target_words) * 3:
                    break

        return None

    match_phrase = _match_phrase_in_cues

    def _download_subtitle_file(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], bool, str]:
        """Fetch soft subtitles directly via raw HTTP VTT text download (0 MB video I/O)."""
        if Path(target_url).exists():
            return (None, False, "en")

        target_dir_path = Path(target_dir) if target_dir else Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

        ydl_opts = {
            "skip_download": True,
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(target_url, download=False) or {}

            subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
            auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

            sub_tracks = subs if subs else auto_subs
            is_auto = not bool(subs) and bool(auto_subs)

            if not sub_tracks:
                return (None, False, "en")

            # Priority language selection
            selected_lang = "en"
            lang_key = next((k for k in ["en", "en-US", "en-orig"] if k in sub_tracks), list(sub_tracks.keys())[0])
            formats = sub_tracks[lang_key]

            vtt_url = next((fmt["url"] for fmt in formats if fmt.get("ext") == "vtt"), None)
            if not vtt_url and formats:
                vtt_url = formats[0].get("url")

            if not vtt_url:
                return (None, False, "en")

            vtt_path = target_dir_path / f"sub_{lang_key}.vtt"
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                vtt_url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)"}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as response, open(vtt_path, "wb") as out_file:
                out_file.write(response.read())

            return (vtt_path, is_auto, lang_key)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed fetching soft subtitle text: %s", exc)
            return (None, False, "en")

    def fetch_subtitles(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, target_dir=target_dir, **kwargs)
        vtt_path = res[0] if isinstance(res, tuple) else res
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return []
        return self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))

    def probe_embedded_subtitles_match(
        self,
        target_url: str,
        target_phrase: str,
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: Optional[float] = None,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, **kwargs)
        vtt_path, detected_is_auto, detected_lang = res if isinstance(res, tuple) else (None, is_auto_generated, track_language)
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return None

        cues = self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))
        if not cues:
            return None

        gap = max_gap_seconds if max_gap_seconds is not None else settings.subtitle_search_padding_seconds
        return self._match_phrase_in_cues(
            target_phrase=target_phrase,
            cues=cues,
            similarity_threshold=similarity_threshold,
            max_gap_seconds=gap,
            track_language=detected_lang,
            is_auto_generated=detected_is_auto,
            **kwargs,
        )

    def probe_metadata(self, url_or_path: str, retries: int = 3, **kwargs) -> VideoMetadata:
        path = Path(url_or_path)
        if path.exists():
            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
            if not cap.isOpened():
                cap.release()
                raise IngestionError(f"Unable to open local video file: {url_or_path}")

            raw_fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
            try:
                fps = float(raw_fps) if raw_fps and not hasattr(raw_fps, "_mock_return_value") and float(raw_fps) > 0 else 25.0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                fps = 25.0

            raw_frames = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)
            try:
                total_frames = int(raw_frames) if raw_frames and not hasattr(raw_frames, "_mock_return_value") else 0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                total_frames = 0

            duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0
            cap.release()

            return VideoMetadata(
                url=str(path),
                duration_seconds=duration,
                fps=fps,
                has_subtitles=False,
                is_local=True,
                stream_path=str(path),
            )

        ydl_opts = {
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "skip_download": True,
            "writesubtitles": False,
            "writeautomaticsub": False,
        }

        for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
            try:
                with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                    info = ydl.extract_info(url_or_path, download=False)
                    if not info:
                        raise IngestionError(f"Could not extract info from URL: {url_or_path}")

                    duration = float(info.get("duration") or 0.0)
                    fps = float(info.get("fps") or 25.0)

                    stream_path = info.get("url")
                    if not stream_path and "formats" in info:
                        formats = info.get("formats", [])
                        for fmt in reversed(formats):
                            if fmt.get("vcodec") != "none" and fmt.get("url"):
                                stream_path = fmt.get("url")
                                break
                        if not stream_path and formats:
                            stream_path = formats[-1].get("url")

                    subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
                    auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

                    return VideoMetadata(
                        url=url_or_path,
                        duration_seconds=duration,
                        fps=fps,
                        has_subtitles=bool(subs or auto_subs),
                        is_local=False,
                        stream_path=stream_path or url_or_path,
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                if attempt == retries:
                    raise IngestionError(f"Probing metadata failed after {retries} attempts: {exc}") from exc
                time.sleep(attempt * 1.5)

    def extract_audio_stream(
        self,
        url_or_path: str,
        output_wav_path: Optional[str] = None,
        job_id: Optional[str] = None,
        output_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
        allow_download: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Path:
        path = Path(url_or_path)

        if path.exists() and path.suffix.lower() in [".wav", ".mp3"] and not output_wav_path and not output_dir:
            return path

        stream_target = self._resolve_direct_stream_url(url_or_path)

        if output_wav_path:
            out_path = Path(output_wav_path)
            target_dir = out_path.parent
        elif output_dir:
            target_dir = Path(output_dir)
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
        else:
            target_dir = Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / (job_id or "default")
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"

        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        if path.exists():
            if path.suffix.lower() == out_path.suffix.lower():
                if path != out_path:
                    shutil.copy(path, out_path)
                return out_path

            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-i", str(path),
                "-vn",
                "-acodec", "libmp3lame" if out_path.suffix.lower() == ".mp3" else "pcm_s16le",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-b:a", "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]
            subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
            return out_path

        # Direct FFmpeg network streaming with HTTP User-Agent and reconnect flags
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-headers", "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)\r\n",
            "-reconnect", "1",
            "-reconnect_streamed", "1",
            "-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
            "-ss", "0",
            "-i", stream_target,
            "-vn",
            "-acodec", "libmp3lame",
            "-ar", "16000",
            "-ac", "1",
            "-b:a", "32k",
            str(out_path),
        ]

        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=180)
            if res.returncode == 0 and out_path.exists() and out_path.stat().st_size > 0:
                return out_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("FFmpeg direct stream audio extraction failed: %s", exc)

        if not allow_download:
            raise IngestionError(f"Direct stream audio extraction failed for {url_or_path} and allow_download=False.")

        raise IngestionError("Audio stream extraction failed.")

    def extract_frame_on_demand(
        self, stream_url: str, timestamp_seconds: float, output_jpg: Path
    ) -> bool:
        stream_target = self._resolve_direct_stream_url(stream_url)
        output_jpg.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-headers", "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)\r\n",
            "-ss", f"{timestamp_seconds:.3f}",
            "-i", stream_target,
            "-vframes", "1",
            "-q:v", "2",
            str(output_jpg),
        ]
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=15)
            return bool(res.returncode == 0 and output_jpg.exists() and output_jpg.stat().st_size > 0)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed on-demand frame capture at %.2fs: %s", timestamp_seconds, exc)
            return False

132. def extract_audio_stream(
    self,
    url_or_path: str,
    output_wav_path: Optional[str] = None,
    job_id: Optional[str] = None,
    output_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
    allow_download: bool = False,
    **kwargs,
) -> Path:

    path = Path(url_or_path)

    # Output location
    if output_wav_path:
        out_path = Path(output_wav_path)
        target_dir = out_path.parent

    elif output_dir:
        target_dir = Path(output_dir)
        out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"

    else:
        target_dir = Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / (job_id or "default")
        out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"


    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


    # Local file
    if path.exists():

        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-i",
            str(path),
            "-vn",
            "-ac",
            "1",
            "-ar",
            "16000",
            "-b:a",
            "32k",
            str(out_path)
        ]

        subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            check=True,
            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL
        )

        return out_path



    #
    # IMPORTANT PART
    # Use yt-dlp only for stream extraction
    # NOT downloading
    #

    ydl_opts = {
        "quiet": True,
        "no_warnings": True,
        "skip_download": True,

        # only audio formats
        "format": "bestaudio/best",

        "http_headers": {
            "User-Agent":
            "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)",
            "Referer":
            "https://ok.ru/"
        }
    }


    try:

        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:

            info = ydl.extract_info(
                url_or_path,
                download=False
            )


            audio_url = info.get("url")


            if not audio_url:

                formats = info.get("formats", [])

                audio_formats = [
                    f for f in formats
                    if f.get("acodec") != "none"
                ]

                if not audio_formats:
                    raise IngestionError(
                        "No audio stream found"
                    )

                audio_url = audio_formats[-1]["url"]



        headers = (
            "Referer: https://ok.ru/\r\n"
            "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0\r\n"
        )


        ffmpeg_cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",

            "-headers",
            headers,

            "-i",
            audio_url,

            "-vn",

            "-ac",
            "1",

            "-ar",
            "16000",

            "-b:a",
            "32k",

            str(out_path)
        ]


        result = subprocess.run(
            ffmpeg_cmd,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=180
        )


        if (
            result.returncode == 0
            and out_path.exists()
            and out_path.stat().st_size > 0
        ):
            return out_path



        logger.error(
            "FFmpeg failed:\n%s",
            result.stderr
        )


    except Exception as exc:

        logger.exception(
            "Audio extraction failed: %s",
            exc
        )



    raise IngestionError(
        "Unable to extract audio stream"
    )

133. import difflib
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union

import cv2
import yt_dlp

from src.config import settings
from src.models.schemas import SubtitleMatchResult, VideoMetadata

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class IngestionError(Exception):
    """Domain exception raised when stream ingestion or metadata probing fails."""
    pass


class SubtitleCue:
    def __init__(self, start_seconds: float, end_seconds: float, text: str):
        self.start_seconds = start_seconds
        self.end_seconds = end_seconds
        self.text = text

    def __getitem__(self, item: str) -> Any:
        if item in ("start_seconds", "start_time", "start"):
            return self.start_seconds
        if item in ("end_seconds", "end_time", "end"):
            return self.end_seconds
        if item in ("text", "matched_text"):
            return self.text
        return getattr(self, item)

    def get(self, item: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        try:
            return self[item]
        except (KeyError, AttributeError):
            return default


def _get_cue_val(cue: Union[dict, SubtitleCue, Any], key: str) -> Any:
    if isinstance(cue, dict):
        if key == "start_seconds":
            return cue.get("start_seconds", cue.get("start_time", cue.get("start")))
        if key == "end_seconds":
            return cue.get("end_seconds", cue.get("end_time", cue.get("end")))
        return cue.get(key)
    try:
        return cue[key]
    except Exception:
        return getattr(cue, key, None)


class StreamIngestionService:
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_text(text: str) -> str:
        text = text.lower()
        text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s]", "", text)
        return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()

    normalize_text = _normalize_text

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_vtt_timestamp(ts_str: str) -> float:
        ts_str = str(ts_str).strip().replace(",", ".")
        parts = ts_str.split(":")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return float(parts[0]) * 3600 + float(parts[1]) * 60 + float(parts[2])
        elif len(parts) == 2:
            return float(parts[0]) * 60 + float(parts[1])
        return float(ts_str)

    parse_vtt_timestamp = _parse_vtt_timestamp

    def _resolve_direct_stream_url(self, url_or_path: str) -> str:
        if not url_or_path.startswith("http") or Path(url_or_path).exists():
            return url_or_path

        direct_stream_indicators = [".m3u8", ".mpd", "vkuser.net", "googlevideo.com", ".mp4", ".ts", "mime="]
        if any(indicator in url_or_path for indicator in direct_stream_indicators):
            return url_or_path

        try:
            meta = self.probe_metadata(url_or_path)
            if meta.stream_path and meta.stream_path.startswith("http"):
                return meta.stream_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Could not resolve direct stream URL for %s: %s", url_or_path, exc)

        return url_or_path

    def _parse_vtt_file(self, file_path: Union[str, Path]) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        path = Path(file_path)
        if not path.exists():
            return []

        cues: List[SubtitleCue] = []
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            lines = f.readlines()

        cue_regex = re.compile(
            r"(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}\s+-->\s+(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}"
        )

        i = 0
        while i < len(lines):
            line = lines[i].strip()
            if "-->" in line and cue_regex.search(line):
                parts = line.split("-->")
                start_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[0].split()[0])
                end_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[1].split()[0])

                text_lines = []
                i += 1
                while i < len(lines) and lines[i].strip() and "-->" not in lines[i]:
                    text_lines.append(lines[i].strip())
                    i += 1

                raw_text = " ".join(text_lines)
                clean_text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", raw_text).strip()
                if clean_text:
                    cues.append(SubtitleCue(start_sec, end_sec, clean_text))
                continue
            i += 1

        return cues

    parse_vtt_file = _parse_vtt_file

    def _match_phrase_in_cues(
        self,
        target_phrase: str,
        cues: List[Union[dict, SubtitleCue]],
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: float = 2.0,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        if not target_phrase or not cues:
            return None

        norm_target = self._normalize_text(target_phrase)
        if not norm_target:
            return None

        target_words = norm_target.split()
        threshold_ratio = similarity_threshold / 100.0 if similarity_threshold > 1.0 else similarity_threshold

        for cue in cues:
            cue_text = _get_cue_val(cue, "text") or ""
            norm_cue = self._normalize_text(cue_text)

            if norm_target in norm_cue:
                return SubtitleMatchResult(
                    start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                    end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                    matched_text=cue_text,
                    similarity_score=1.0,
                    track_language=track_language,
                    is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                )

            if norm_cue:
                score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, norm_cue).ratio()
                if score >= threshold_ratio and len(target_words) <= len(norm_cue.split()) + 2:
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                        end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                        matched_text=cue_text,
                        similarity_score=score,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

        for start_idx in range(len(cues)):
            accumulated_words = []
            accumulated_raw_text = []
            start_time = float(_get_cue_val(cues[start_idx], "start_seconds"))

            for current_idx in range(start_idx, len(cues)):
                curr_cue = cues[current_idx]

                if current_idx > start_idx:
                    prev_cue = cues[current_idx - 1]
                    gap = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "start_seconds")) - float(_get_cue_val(prev_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    if gap > max_gap_seconds:
                        break

                curr_text = _get_cue_val(curr_cue, "text") or ""
                norm_words = self._normalize_text(curr_text).split()
                accumulated_words.extend(norm_words)
                accumulated_raw_text.append(curr_text)

                accumulated_phrase = " ".join(accumulated_words)

                if norm_target in accumulated_phrase:
                    end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=start_time,
                        end_time=end_time,
                        matched_text=full_matched_text,
                        similarity_score=1.0,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

                if accumulated_phrase:
                    score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, accumulated_phrase).ratio()
                    if score >= threshold_ratio and abs(len(accumulated_words) - len(target_words)) <= 3:
                        end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                        full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                        return SubtitleMatchResult(
                            start_time=start_time,
                            end_time=end_time,
                            matched_text=full_matched_text,
                            similarity_score=score,
                            track_language=track_language,
                            is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                        )

                if len(accumulated_words) > len(target_words) * 3:
                    break

        return None

    match_phrase = _match_phrase_in_cues

    def _download_subtitle_file(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], bool, str]:
        if Path(target_url).exists():
            return (None, False, "en")

        target_dir_path = Path(target_dir) if target_dir else Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

        ydl_opts = {
            "skip_download": True,
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(target_url, download=False) or {}

            subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
            auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

            sub_tracks = subs if subs else auto_subs
            is_auto = not bool(subs) and bool(auto_subs)

            if not sub_tracks:
                return (None, False, "en")

            lang_key = next((k for k in ["en", "en-US", "en-orig"] if k in sub_tracks), list(sub_tracks.keys())[0])
            formats = sub_tracks[lang_key]

            vtt_url = next((fmt["url"] for fmt in formats if fmt.get("ext") == "vtt"), None)
            if not vtt_url and formats:
                vtt_url = formats[0].get("url")

            if not vtt_url:
                return (None, False, "en")

            vtt_path = target_dir_path / f"sub_{lang_key}.vtt"
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                vtt_url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)", "Referer": "https://ok.ru/"}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as response, open(vtt_path, "wb") as out_file:
                out_file.write(response.read())

            return (vtt_path, is_auto, lang_key)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed fetching soft subtitle text: %s", exc)
            return (None, False, "en")

    def fetch_subtitles(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, target_dir=target_dir, **kwargs)
        vtt_path = res[0] if isinstance(res, tuple) else res
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return []
        return self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))

    def probe_embedded_subtitles_match(
        self,
        target_url: str,
        target_phrase: str,
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: Optional[float] = None,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, **kwargs)
        vtt_path, detected_is_auto, detected_lang = res if isinstance(res, tuple) else (None, is_auto_generated, track_language)
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return None

        cues = self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))
        if not cues:
            return None

        gap = max_gap_seconds if max_gap_seconds is not None else settings.subtitle_search_padding_seconds
        return self._match_phrase_in_cues(
            target_phrase=target_phrase,
            cues=cues,
            similarity_threshold=similarity_threshold,
            max_gap_seconds=gap,
            track_language=detected_lang,
            is_auto_generated=detected_is_auto,
            **kwargs,
        )

    def probe_metadata(self, url_or_path: str, retries: int = 3, **kwargs) -> VideoMetadata:
        path = Path(url_or_path)
        if path.exists():
            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
            if not cap.isOpened():
                cap.release()
                raise IngestionError(f"Unable to open local video file: {url_or_path}")

            raw_fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
            try:
                fps = float(raw_fps) if raw_fps and not hasattr(raw_fps, "_mock_return_value") and float(raw_fps) > 0 else 25.0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                fps = 25.0

            raw_frames = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)
            try:
                total_frames = int(raw_frames) if raw_frames and not hasattr(raw_frames, "_mock_return_value") else 0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                total_frames = 0

            duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0
            cap.release()

            return VideoMetadata(
                url=str(path),
                duration_seconds=duration,
                fps=fps,
                has_subtitles=False,
                is_local=True,
                stream_path=str(path),
            )

        ydl_opts = {
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "skip_download": True,
            "writesubtitles": False,
            "writeautomaticsub": False,
        }

        for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
            try:
                with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                    info = ydl.extract_info(url_or_path, download=False)
                    if not info:
                        raise IngestionError(f"Could not extract info from URL: {url_or_path}")

                    duration = float(info.get("duration") or 0.0)
                    fps = float(info.get("fps") or 25.0)

                    stream_path = info.get("url")
                    if not stream_path and "formats" in info:
                        formats = info.get("formats", [])
                        for fmt in reversed(formats):
                            if fmt.get("vcodec") != "none" and fmt.get("url"):
                                stream_path = fmt.get("url")
                                break
                        if not stream_path and formats:
                            stream_path = formats[-1].get("url")

                    subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
                    auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

                    return VideoMetadata(
                        url=url_or_path,
                        duration_seconds=duration,
                        fps=fps,
                        has_subtitles=bool(subs or auto_subs),
                        is_local=False,
                        stream_path=stream_path or url_or_path,
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                if attempt == retries:
                    raise IngestionError(f"Probing metadata failed after {retries} attempts: {exc}") from exc
                time.sleep(attempt * 1.5)

    def download_video_cache(self, url: str, job_id: str, output_dir: Optional[Path] = None) -> Path:
        path = Path(url)
        if path.exists():
            return path

        target_dir = output_dir or Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / job_id
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output_mp4 = target_dir / f"{job_id}_video.mp4"

        if output_mp4.exists() and output_mp4.stat().st_size > 0:
            return output_mp4

        ydl_opts = {
            "format": "worstvideo[ext=mp4]+worstaudio[ext=m4a]/worst[ext=mp4]/worst",
            "outtmpl": str(output_mp4),
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                ydl.download([url])

            if output_mp4.exists() and output_mp4.stat().st_size > 0:
                return output_mp4
        except Exception as exc:
            raise IngestionError(f"Failed to download video stream cache for {url}: {exc}") from exc

        raise IngestionError("Video cache file missing or empty after download.")

    def extract_audio_stream(
        self,
        url_or_path: str,
        output_wav_path: Optional[str] = None,
        job_id: Optional[str] = None,
        output_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
        allow_download: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Path:
        path = Path(url_or_path)

        # Output location
        if output_wav_path:
            out_path = Path(output_wav_path)
            target_dir = out_path.parent
        elif output_dir:
            target_dir = Path(output_dir)
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
        else:
            target_dir = Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / (job_id or "default")
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"

        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # Local file
        if path.exists():
            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-i", str(path),
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-b:a", "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]
            subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                check=True,
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            )
            return out_path

        # Stream extraction using yt-dlp metadata probe
        ydl_opts = {
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "skip_download": True,
            "format": "bestaudio/best",
            "http_headers": {
                "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)",
                "Referer": "https://ok.ru/",
            },
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(url_or_path, download=False)
                audio_url = info.get("url")

                if not audio_url:
                    formats = info.get("formats", [])
                    audio_formats = [
                        f for f in formats if f.get("acodec") != "none"
                    ]

                    if not audio_formats:
                        raise IngestionError("No audio stream found")

                    audio_url = audio_formats[-1]["url"]

            headers = (
                "Referer: https://ok.ru/\r\n"
                "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0\r\n"
            )

            ffmpeg_cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-headers", headers,
                "-i", audio_url,
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-b:a", "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]

            result = subprocess.run(
                ffmpeg_cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=180,
            )

            if (
                result.returncode == 0
                and out_path.exists()
                and out_path.stat().st_size > 0
            ):
                return out_path

            logger.error("FFmpeg failed:\n%s", result.stderr)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Audio extraction failed: %s", exc)

        raise IngestionError("Unable to extract audio stream")

    def extract_frame_on_demand(
        self, stream_url: str, timestamp_seconds: float, output_jpg: Path
    ) -> bool:
        stream_target = self._resolve_direct_stream_url(stream_url)
        output_jpg.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-headers", headers,
            "-ss", f"{timestamp_seconds:.3f}",
            "-i", stream_target,
            "-vframes", "1",
            "-q:v", "2",

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed on-demand frame capture at %.2fs: %s", timestamp_seconds, exc)
            return False
