134. I checked this version too. This one is **better than your previous `extract_audio_stream` implementation**, but it still has the same root problem with OK.ru/VK direct streams.

Your failure:

```
IngestionError: Direct stream audio extraction failed ...
```

happens because the URL returned:

```
https://ok6-14.vkuser.net/.../video/
```

is **not an audio-only stream**.

It is a **video CDN stream**. Your code now does:

```python
audio_formats = [
    f
    for f in formats
    if (
        f.get("acodec") != "none"
        and f.get("vcodec") == "none"
    )
]
```

This is the critical part.

For OK.ru, many videos do **not expose separate audio-only formats**. They expose:

```
format:
    vcodec = h264
    acodec = aac
```

meaning:

```
video + audio combined stream
```

So your filter removes them.

Result:

```python
if not audio_formats:
    raise IngestionError("No pure audio stream available")
```

Then extraction dies.

---

## What you should change

Do not require:

```python
f.get("vcodec") == "none"
```

Instead choose any format containing audio.

Replace:

```python
audio_formats = [
    f
    for f in formats
    if (
        f.get("acodec") != "none"
        and f.get("vcodec") == "none"
    )
]
```

with:

```python
audio_formats = [
    f
    for f in formats
    if f.get("acodec") and f.get("acodec") != "none"
]
```

Now it accepts:

```
video+audio
```

streams.

---

## Also this line is risky

```python
audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: x.get("abr") or 0,
    reverse=True,
)
```

For combined streams:

```
abr
```

is often missing.

You should sort by bitrate fallback:

```python
audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x.get("abr")
        or x.get("tbr")
        or 0
    ),
    reverse=True,
)
```

---

## Bigger issue: you are extracting audio from the wrong URL

Your earlier:

```python
meta.stream_path
```

returned:

```
vkuser.net/.../video/
```

Then you directly send it to ffmpeg.

For OK.ru, the safest flow is:

```
OK.ru URL
     |
     |
 yt-dlp
     |
     |
 formats[]
     |
     |
 pick format with audio
     |
     |
 ffmpeg -vn
     |
     |
 mp3/wav
```

Do NOT use:

```
probe_metadata()
      |
      |
stream_path
      |
      |
ffmpeg
```

because `stream_path` is usually the first playable video stream, not necessarily the best audio source.

---

## Modify your extraction section like this

Replace everything from:

```python
formats = info.get("formats", [])

audio_formats = [
...
]
```

with:

```python
formats = info.get("formats", [])

audio_formats = [
    f for f in formats
    if f.get("acodec") 
    and f.get("acodec") != "none"
]

if not audio_formats:
    raise IngestionError("No audio stream available")

audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x.get("abr")
        or x.get("tbr")
        or 0
    ),
    reverse=True
)

selected = audio_formats[0]

audio_url = selected["url"]

logger.info(
    "Selected format: id=%s ext=%s acodec=%s vcodec=%s",
    selected.get("format_id"),
    selected.get("ext"),
    selected.get("acodec"),
    selected.get("vcodec")
)
```

---

## One more important fix

Your ffmpeg command:

```python
"-headers", headers,
"-i", audio_url,
```

should include reconnect flags because VK CDN URLs expire or disconnect:

```python
ffmpeg_cmd = [
    "ffmpeg",
    "-y",
    "-headers", headers,
    "-reconnect", "1",
    "-reconnect_streamed", "1",
    "-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
    "-i", audio_url,
    "-vn",
    "-ac", "1",
    "-ar", "16000",
    "-b:a", "32k",
    str(out_path),
]
```

---

## Your current version ranking:

### Version 1

❌ Uses resolved `stream_path`
❌ Often gets video-only CDN URL
❌ Fails

### Version 2

✅ Uses yt-dlp
✅ Better approach
❌ Only accepts pure audio streams
❌ OK.ru often has muxed streams

### Version 3 (after above patch)

✅ Uses yt-dlp
✅ Handles muxed video+audio
✅ Lets ffmpeg strip video
✅ Much more likely to work

---

One thing I would test next:

Run:

```python
service = StreamIngestionService()

service.extract_audio_stream(
    "https://ok.ru/video/248244667877",
    job_id="test"
)
```

and print the selected format log:

```
Selected format:
id=...
ext=...
acodec=aac
vcodec=h264
```

If you see that, the pipeline is finally selecting the correct stream.


135. import difflib
import logging
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union

import cv2
import yt_dlp

from src.config import settings
from src.models.schemas import SubtitleMatchResult, VideoMetadata

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class IngestionError(Exception):
    """Domain exception raised when stream ingestion or metadata probing fails."""
    pass


class SubtitleCue:
    def __init__(self, start_seconds: float, end_seconds: float, text: str):
        self.start_seconds = start_seconds
        self.end_seconds = end_seconds
        self.text = text

    def __getitem__(self, item: str) -> Any:
        if item in ("start_seconds", "start_time", "start"):
            return self.start_seconds
        if item in ("end_seconds", "end_time", "end"):
            return self.end_seconds
        if item in ("text", "matched_text"):
            return self.text
        return getattr(self, item)

    def get(self, item: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        try:
            return self[item]
        except (KeyError, AttributeError):
            return default


def _get_cue_val(cue: Union[dict, SubtitleCue, Any], key: str) -> Any:
    if isinstance(cue, dict):
        if key == "start_seconds":
            return cue.get("start_seconds", cue.get("start_time", cue.get("start")))
        if key == "end_seconds":
            return cue.get("end_seconds", cue.get("end_time", cue.get("end")))
        return cue.get(key)
    try:
        return cue[key]
    except Exception:
        return getattr(cue, key, None)


class StreamIngestionService:
    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_text(text: str) -> str:
        text = text.lower()
        text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s]", "", text)
        return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()

    normalize_text = _normalize_text

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_vtt_timestamp(ts_str: str) -> float:
        ts_str = str(ts_str).strip().replace(",", ".")
        parts = ts_str.split(":")
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return float(parts[0]) * 3600 + float(parts[1]) * 60 + float(parts[2])
        elif len(parts) == 2:
            return float(parts[0]) * 60 + float(parts[1])
        return float(ts_str)

    parse_vtt_timestamp = _parse_vtt_timestamp

    def _resolve_direct_stream_url(self, url_or_path: str) -> str:
        if not url_or_path.startswith("http") or Path(url_or_path).exists():
            return url_or_path

        direct_stream_indicators = [".m3u8", ".mpd", "vkuser.net", "googlevideo.com", ".mp4", ".ts", "mime="]
        if any(indicator in url_or_path for indicator in direct_stream_indicators):
            return url_or_path

        try:
            meta = self.probe_metadata(url_or_path)
            if meta.stream_path and meta.stream_path.startswith("http"):
                return meta.stream_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Could not resolve direct stream URL for %s: %s", url_or_path, exc)

        return url_or_path

    def _parse_vtt_file(self, file_path: Union[str, Path]) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        path = Path(file_path)
        if not path.exists():
            return []

        cues: List[SubtitleCue] = []
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            lines = f.readlines()

        cue_regex = re.compile(
            r"(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}\s+-->\s+(\d{2}:)?\d{2}:\d{2}[\.,]\d{3}"
        )

        i = 0
        while i < len(lines):
            line = lines[i].strip()
            if "-->" in line and cue_regex.search(line):
                parts = line.split("-->")
                start_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[0].split()[0])
                end_sec = self._parse_vtt_timestamp(parts[1].split()[0])

                text_lines = []
                i += 1
                while i < len(lines) and lines[i].strip() and "-->" not in lines[i]:
                    text_lines.append(lines[i].strip())
                    i += 1

                raw_text = " ".join(text_lines)
                clean_text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", raw_text).strip()
                if clean_text:
                    cues.append(SubtitleCue(start_sec, end_sec, clean_text))
                continue
            i += 1

        return cues

    parse_vtt_file = _parse_vtt_file

    def _match_phrase_in_cues(
        self,
        target_phrase: str,
        cues: List[Union[dict, SubtitleCue]],
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: float = 2.0,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        if not target_phrase or not cues:
            return None

        norm_target = self._normalize_text(target_phrase)
        if not norm_target:
            return None

        target_words = norm_target.split()
        threshold_ratio = similarity_threshold / 100.0 if similarity_threshold > 1.0 else similarity_threshold

        for cue in cues:
            cue_text = _get_cue_val(cue, "text") or ""
            norm_cue = self._normalize_text(cue_text)

            if norm_target in norm_cue:
                return SubtitleMatchResult(
                    start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                    end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                    matched_text=cue_text,
                    similarity_score=1.0,
                    track_language=track_language,
                    is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                )

            if norm_cue:
                score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, norm_cue).ratio()
                if score >= threshold_ratio and len(target_words) <= len(norm_cue.split()) + 2:
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "start_seconds")),
                        end_time=float(_get_cue_val(cue, "end_seconds")),
                        matched_text=cue_text,
                        similarity_score=score,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

        for start_idx in range(len(cues)):
            accumulated_words = []
            accumulated_raw_text = []
            start_time = float(_get_cue_val(cues[start_idx], "start_seconds"))

            for current_idx in range(start_idx, len(cues)):
                curr_cue = cues[current_idx]

                if current_idx > start_idx:
                    prev_cue = cues[current_idx - 1]
                    gap = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "start_seconds")) - float(_get_cue_val(prev_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    if gap > max_gap_seconds:
                        break

                curr_text = _get_cue_val(curr_cue, "text") or ""
                norm_words = self._normalize_text(curr_text).split()
                accumulated_words.extend(norm_words)
                accumulated_raw_text.append(curr_text)

                accumulated_phrase = " ".join(accumulated_words)

                if norm_target in accumulated_phrase:
                    end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                    full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                    return SubtitleMatchResult(
                        start_time=start_time,
                        end_time=end_time,
                        matched_text=full_matched_text,
                        similarity_score=1.0,
                        track_language=track_language,
                        is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                    )

                if accumulated_phrase:
                    score = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, norm_target, accumulated_phrase).ratio()
                    if score >= threshold_ratio and abs(len(accumulated_words) - len(target_words)) <= 3:
                        end_time = float(_get_cue_val(curr_cue, "end_seconds"))
                        full_matched_text = " ".join(accumulated_raw_text)
                        return SubtitleMatchResult(
                            start_time=start_time,
                            end_time=end_time,
                            matched_text=full_matched_text,
                            similarity_score=score,
                            track_language=track_language,
                            is_auto_generated=is_auto_generated,
                        )

                if len(accumulated_words) > len(target_words) * 3:
                    break

        return None

    match_phrase = _match_phrase_in_cues

    def _download_subtitle_file(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], bool, str]:
        if Path(target_url).exists():
            return (None, False, "en")

        target_dir_path = Path(target_dir) if target_dir else Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())

        ydl_opts = {
            "skip_download": True,
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(target_url, download=False) or {}

            subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
            auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

            sub_tracks = subs if subs else auto_subs
            is_auto = not bool(subs) and bool(auto_subs)

            if not sub_tracks:
                return (None, False, "en")

            lang_key = next((k for k in ["en", "en-US", "en-orig"] if k in sub_tracks), list(sub_tracks.keys())[0])
            formats = sub_tracks[lang_key]

            vtt_url = next((fmt["url"] for fmt in formats if fmt.get("ext") == "vtt"), None)
            if not vtt_url and formats:
                vtt_url = formats[0].get("url")

            if not vtt_url:
                return (None, False, "en")

            vtt_path = target_dir_path / f"sub_{lang_key}.vtt"
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                vtt_url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)", "Referer": "https://ok.ru/"}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as response, open(vtt_path, "wb") as out_file:
                out_file.write(response.read())

            return (vtt_path, is_auto, lang_key)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed fetching soft subtitle text: %s", exc)
            return (None, False, "en")

    def fetch_subtitles(
        self, target_url: str, target_dir: Optional[Path] = None, **kwargs
    ) -> List[SubtitleCue]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, target_dir=target_dir, **kwargs)
        vtt_path = res[0] if isinstance(res, tuple) else res
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return []
        return self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))

    def probe_embedded_subtitles_match(
        self,
        target_url: str,
        target_phrase: str,
        similarity_threshold: float = 85.0,
        max_gap_seconds: Optional[float] = None,
        track_language: str = "en",
        is_auto_generated: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Optional[SubtitleMatchResult]:
        res = self._download_subtitle_file(target_url, **kwargs)
        vtt_path, detected_is_auto, detected_lang = res if isinstance(res, tuple) else (None, is_auto_generated, track_language)
        if not vtt_path or not Path(vtt_path).exists():
            return None

        cues = self._parse_vtt_file(str(vtt_path))
        if not cues:
            return None

        gap = max_gap_seconds if max_gap_seconds is not None else settings.subtitle_search_padding_seconds
        return self._match_phrase_in_cues(
            target_phrase=target_phrase,
            cues=cues,
            similarity_threshold=similarity_threshold,
            max_gap_seconds=gap,
            track_language=detected_lang,
            is_auto_generated=detected_is_auto,
            **kwargs,
        )

    def probe_metadata(self, url_or_path: str, retries: int = 3, **kwargs) -> VideoMetadata:
        path = Path(url_or_path)
        if path.exists():
            cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(path))
            if not cap.isOpened():
                cap.release()
                raise IngestionError(f"Unable to open local video file: {url_or_path}")

            raw_fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
            try:
                fps = float(raw_fps) if raw_fps and not hasattr(raw_fps, "_mock_return_value") and float(raw_fps) > 0 else 25.0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                fps = 25.0

            raw_frames = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT)
            try:
                total_frames = int(raw_frames) if raw_frames and not hasattr(raw_frames, "_mock_return_value") else 0
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                total_frames = 0

            duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0
            cap.release()

            return VideoMetadata(
                url=str(path),
                duration_seconds=duration,
                fps=fps,
                has_subtitles=False,
                is_local=True,
                stream_path=str(path),
            )

        ydl_opts = {
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "skip_download": True,
            "writesubtitles": False,
            "writeautomaticsub": False,
        }

        for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
            try:
                with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                    info = ydl.extract_info(url_or_path, download=False)
                    if not info:
                        raise IngestionError(f"Could not extract info from URL: {url_or_path}")

                    duration = float(info.get("duration") or 0.0)
                    fps = float(info.get("fps") or 25.0)

                    stream_path = info.get("url")
                    if not stream_path and "formats" in info:
                        formats = info.get("formats", [])
                        for fmt in reversed(formats):
                            if fmt.get("vcodec") != "none" and fmt.get("url"):
                                stream_path = fmt.get("url")
                                break
                        if not stream_path and formats:
                            stream_path = formats[-1].get("url")

                    subs = info.get("subtitles") or {}
                    auto_subs = info.get("automatic_captions") or {}

                    return VideoMetadata(
                        url=url_or_path,
                        duration_seconds=duration,
                        fps=fps,
                        has_subtitles=bool(subs or auto_subs),
                        is_local=False,
                        stream_path=stream_path or url_or_path,
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                if attempt == retries:
                    raise IngestionError(f"Probing metadata failed after {retries} attempts: {exc}") from exc
                time.sleep(attempt * 1.5)

    def download_video_cache(self, url: str, job_id: str, output_dir: Optional[Path] = None) -> Path:
        path = Path(url)
        if path.exists():
            return path

        target_dir = output_dir or Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / job_id
        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        output_mp4 = target_dir / f"{job_id}_video.mp4"

        if output_mp4.exists() and output_mp4.stat().st_size > 0:
            return output_mp4

        ydl_opts = {
            "format": "worstvideo[ext=mp4]+worstaudio[ext=m4a]/worst[ext=mp4]/worst",
            "outtmpl": str(output_mp4),
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                ydl.download([url])

            if output_mp4.exists() and output_mp4.stat().st_size > 0:
                return output_mp4
        except Exception as exc:
            raise IngestionError(f"Failed to download video stream cache for {url}: {exc}") from exc

        raise IngestionError("Video cache file missing or empty after download.")

    def extract_audio_stream(
        self,
        url_or_path: str,
        output_wav_path: Optional[str] = None,
        job_id: Optional[str] = None,
        output_dir: Optional[Path] = None,
        allow_download: bool = False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> Path:
        path = Path(url_or_path)

        # Output location
        if output_wav_path:
            out_path = Path(output_wav_path)
            target_dir = out_path.parent
        elif output_dir:
            target_dir = Path(output_dir)
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
        else:
            target_dir = Path(settings.artifact_storage_dir) / (job_id or "default")
            out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"

        target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # Local file
        if path.exists():
            cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-i", str(path),
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-b:a", "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]
            subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                check=True,
                stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            )
            return out_path

        # Stream extraction using yt-dlp format filtering
        ydl_opts = {
            "quiet": True,
            "no_warnings": True,
            "skip_download": True,
            "format": "bestaudio[acodec!=none]/best",
            "http_headers": {
                "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)",
                "Referer": "https://ok.ru/",
            },
        }

        try:
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(ydl_opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(url_or_path, download=False)
                formats = info.get("formats", [])

                # Accept streams containing audio (muxed or standalone)
                audio_formats = [
                    f for f in formats
                    if f.get("acodec") and f.get("acodec") != "none"
                ]

                if not audio_formats:
                    raise IngestionError("No audio stream available")

                # Rank streams prioritizing audio bitrate with total bitrate fallback
                audio_formats.sort(
                    key=lambda x: (
                        x.get("abr")
                        or x.get("tbr")
                        or 0
                    ),
                    reverse=True,
                )

                selected = audio_formats[0]
                audio_url = selected["url"]

                logger.info(
                    "Selected format: id=%s ext=%s acodec=%s vcodec=%s",
                    selected.get("format_id"),
                    selected.get("ext"),
                    selected.get("acodec"),
                    selected.get("vcodec"),
                )

            print("Selected audio URL:")
            print(audio_url[:300])

            headers = (
                "Referer: https://ok.ru/\r\n"
                "User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0\r\n"
            )

            ffmpeg_cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-headers", headers,
                "-reconnect", "1",
                "-reconnect_streamed", "1",
                "-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
                "-i", audio_url,
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-b:a", "32k",
                str(out_path),
            ]

            result = subprocess.run(
                ffmpeg_cmd,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=180,
            )

            if (
                result.returncode == 0
                and out_path.exists()
                and out_path.stat().st_size > 0
            ):
                return out_path

            logger.error("FFmpeg failed:\n%s", result.stderr)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Audio extraction failed: %s", exc)

        raise IngestionError("Unable to extract audio stream")

    def extract_frame_on_demand(
        self, stream_url: str, timestamp_seconds: float, output_jpg: Path
    ) -> bool:
        stream_target = self._resolve_direct_stream_url(stream_url)
        output_jpg.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        headers = "Referer: https://ok.ru/\r\nUser-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)\r\n"
        cmd = [
            "ffmpeg",
            "-y",
            "-headers", headers,
            "-reconnect", "1",
            "-reconnect_streamed", "1",
            "-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
            "-ss", f"{timestamp_seconds:.3f}",
            "-i", stream_target,
            "-vframes", "1",
            "-q:v", "2",
            str(output_jpg),
        ]
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            return bool(res.returncode == 0 and output_jpg.exists() and output_jpg.stat().st_size > 0)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning("Failed on-demand frame capture at %.2fs: %s", timestamp_seconds, exc)
            return False



what about this as well. 

136. I reviewed this third version too. Compared with the previous two versions, this is actually the strongest version of extract_audio_stream() and extract_frame_on_demand(), but there are still some important issues.
I'll focus on the changes and whether this is production-ready.
✅ Improvements in this version
1. Better audio format selection
Previous:

audio_formats = [
    f for f in formats
    if f.get("acodec") != "none"
]
Problem:

This could select video+audio streams.
Example:
vcodec=h264
acodec=opus
Then ffmpeg receives a video stream and you discard it with:

-vn
Wasteful and sometimes fails.
Your new logic:

audio_formats = [
    f for f in formats
    if f.get("acodec") and f.get("acodec") != "none"
]
This is more flexible.
It accepts:

audio only:
vcodec=none
acodec=opus
and:

muxed:
vcodec=h264
acodec=aac
Good.
⚠️ But the sorting can still choose the wrong stream
You have:

audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x.get("abr")
        or x.get("tbr")
        or 0
    ),
    reverse=True,
)
Problem:
tbr is total bitrate.
Example:

Format A:
video=1000kbps
audio=128kbps
tbr=1128

Format B:
audio only
abr=160kbps
tbr=160
Your code chooses A.
Then ffmpeg downloads unnecessary video data.
I would slightly modify:

audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x.get("vcodec") == "none",
        x.get("abr") or 0
    ),
    reverse=True,
)
This prioritizes:

Pure audio
Highest audio bitrate
2. Reconnect flags are a good addition
You added:

"-reconnect", "1",
"-reconnect_streamed", "1",
"-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
Good.
For unstable streaming sources this helps.
Especially:

OK.ru
Google video CDN
HLS streams
3. Logging improvement
Good:

logger.info(
    "Selected format: id=%s ext=%s acodec=%s vcodec=%s",
)
This is much better than debugging with:

print()
But you still have:

print("Selected audio URL:")
print(audio_url[:300])
Remove this.
Use:

logger.debug("Audio URL: %s", audio_url[:300])
Otherwise production logs get messy.
Bigger issue I noticed
❌ allow_download is unused
Your function:

def extract_audio_stream(
    ...
    allow_download: bool = False,
)
but nowhere:

if allow_download:
exists.
So currently:

allow_download=True
allow_download=False
behave exactly the same.
Either remove it or implement it.
Important bug possibility
Here:

path = Path(url_or_path)
For URLs:

https://ok.ru/video/12345
Python creates:

Path("https:/ok.ru/video/12345")
Usually .exists() returns false, so okay.
But for some weird URLs:

file://...
or local paths containing URL-like characters, this can behave unexpectedly.
Better:

if Path(url_or_path).exists() and not url_or_path.startswith("http"):
Audio output naming issue
You call:

out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id or 'default'}_audio.mp3"
but ffmpeg:

-b:a 32k
and:

-ar 16000
This is actually speech extraction.
For ASR pipelines, better:

.wav
because models like:

Whisper
wav2vec2
HuBERT
usually expect PCM WAV.
I would change:

out_path = target_dir / f"{job_id}_audio.wav"
and ffmpeg:

-f wav
-acodec pcm_s16le
Example:

ffmpeg_cmd = [
    "ffmpeg",
    "-y",
    "-headers", headers,
    "-i", audio_url,
    "-vn",
    "-ac", "1",
    "-ar", "16000",
    "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
    str(out_path),
]
Much better for ML pipelines.
Frame extraction
This:

"-ss", f"{timestamp_seconds:.3f}",
"-i", stream_target,
works, but ordering matters.
Current:

seek after input open
means accurate but slower.
For random frame extraction:
Better:

ffmpeg
-ss timestamp
-i stream
which you already have.
Good.
But missing:
-threads 1
For many concurrent jobs, ffmpeg can consume lots of CPU.
For a backend service:

"-threads", "1"
might be useful.
Security / reliability issue
You trust:

audio_url = selected["url"]
Then pass directly:

ffmpeg -i audio_url
If the URL contains special characters, headers, etc., failures can happen.
Not a huge issue because subprocess uses list arguments, not shell.
Safe.

137. I reviewed this final version. This is very close to production quality, and the changes you applied address almost all the issues from the previous review.
I would approve this with a few small corrections before merging.
✅ What is now correctly fixed
1. Pure audio prioritization
You changed:

audio_formats.sort(
    key=lambda x: (
        x.get("vcodec") == "none",
        x.get("abr") or 0,
    ),
    reverse=True,
)
This is good.
Priority becomes:

vcodec == none
highest audio bitrate
Example:

Format A:
vcodec=none
abr=128

Format B:
vcodec=h264
abr=320
A wins.
That is exactly what you want for ASR extraction.
2. WAV output
You changed:

_audio.mp3
to:

_audio.wav
and:

-acodec pcm_s16le
Excellent.
For:

Whisper
wav2vec2
HuBERT
speech embeddings
audio classifiers
this is the right format:

PCM
16kHz
mono
16-bit
3. Thread limiting
Added:

"-threads", "1"
Good for a backend worker environment.
Without this, 10 parallel jobs could make ffmpeg consume every CPU core.
4. allow_download implementation
This is now meaningful:

if allow_download:
    cached_video = self.download_video_cache(...)
Good fallback strategy:

Try direct stream
        |
        |
        fail
        |
        |
Download
        |
        |
Extract audio
This is exactly how resilient media pipelines usually behave.
5. URL/path guards
This is much safer:

is_local = not url_or_path.startswith("http") and Path(url_or_path).exists()
Good.
⚠️ Remaining issues
Issue 1 (important): _resolve_direct_stream_url condition is confusing
Current:

if url_or_path.startswith("http") is False or (not url_or_path.startswith("http") and Path(url_or_path).exists()):
    return url_or_path
The second half is redundant.
Because:

url_or_path.startswith("http") is False
already covers non-http paths.
Simplify:

if not url_or_path.startswith("http"):
    return url_or_path
Cleaner.
Issue 2: yt_dlp format option is slightly misleading
You have:

"format": "bestaudio[acodec!=none]/best"
Then later:

formats = info.get("formats", [])
The format selection does not really matter because you manually select from formats.
I would remove:

"format"
completely:

ydl_opts = {
    "quiet": True,
    "no_warnings": True,
    "skip_download": True,
}
This avoids yt-dlp filtering away formats before your ranking logic.
Issue 3: download_video_cache() format is not ideal
Current:

"worstvideo[ext=mp4]+worstaudio[ext=m4a]/worst[ext=mp4]/worst"
For audio extraction fallback, downloading the worst video is actually okay, but it may create unnecessary problems.
A better fallback:

"format": "worstaudio/worst"
because you only need audio.
However, if the source has no audio-only stream, then your current fallback is safer.
So this depends on your target sources.
For OK.ru specifically, your current approach may be more reliable.
Issue 4: logger.debug(audio_url[:300])
This is okay:

logger.debug("Selected audio URL: %s", audio_url[:300])
but remember these URLs often contain:

tokens
expiry timestamps
signatures
Example:

https://cdn.xyz/video?token=abc123....
Even debug logs can leak credentials.
Safer:

from urllib.parse import urlparse

parsed = urlparse(audio_url)

logger.debug(
    "Selected audio stream host=%s path=%s",
    parsed.netloc,
    parsed.path
)
Issue 5: Subtitle temp directory cleanup
Here:

target_dir_path = Path(target_dir) if target_dir else Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
If no target_dir is passed:
you create:

/tmp/tmpxxxx/sub_en.vtt
but never delete it.
For long-running workers:
1000 jobs = 1000 temp folders.
Add:

import shutil
and after processing:

shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
or use:

TemporaryDirectory()
context manager.
Issue 6: Missing ffmpeg overwrite safety
You have:

-y
which overwrites existing files.
For job retries, that may be okay.
But if two workers process the same job:
Worker A:

writes audio.wav
Worker B:

overwrites audio.wav
Consider job locking if running distributed workers.
Not an ingestion.py issue though.

139. I checked your updated `ingestion.py` logic against the error you got.

The good news: **your latest change fixed one problem** (you no longer force `yt_dlp` on already-resolved `vkuser.net` URLs). But the failure is now happening because the **pipeline is still not treating the URL correctly downstream**.

Let's debug step-by-step.

is already a direct stream.

```
        |

        |
        v
HTTP 400
```

because yt-dlp thinks it is a webpage.

is correct.

```python
        ".m3u8",
        ".mpd",
        "vkuser.net",
        "googlevideo.com",
        ".mp4",
        ".ts",
        "mime="
    ]
```

and failing.

instead of:

```python
def _is_direct_stream(self, url):
```

# The correct flow should be:

```
Original OK.ru URL
        |

        v
yt-dlp metadata extraction
        |

formats[]
        |

        +---- video stream
        |
        +---- audio stream
                 |
                 |
                 v
             ffmpeg
                 |
                 v
              wav
```

---

# Fix 1: Do NOT treat vkuser.net as direct audio

Change:

```python
def _is_direct_stream(url: str) -> bool:
```

from:

```python
direct_stream_indicators = [
    ".m3u8",
    ".mpd",
    "vkuser.net",
    "googlevideo.com",
    ".ts",
    ".mp4",
    "mime=",
]
```

to:

```python
direct_stream_indicators = [
    ".m3u8",
    ".mpd",
    ".ts",
]
```

Remove:

```python
"vkuser.net",
"googlevideo.com",
".mp4",
"mime="
```

because those are often **resolved video URLs**, not guaranteed audio.

---

# Fix 2: Add audio format validation

Your current code:

```python
audio_formats = [
    f for f in formats
    if f.get("acodec") and f.get("acodec") != "none"
]
```

Good.

But add:

```python
if not audio_formats:
    logger.warning("No separate audio stream found")
```

because some sites provide only muxed video.

---

# Fix 3: Use yt-dlp to resolve OK.ru first

Your URL:

```
https://ok6-14.vkuser.net/...
```

is already expired/temporary.

You should store:

```
OK.ru page URL
```

not:

```
vkuser.net CDN URL
```

Example:

Good:

```
https://ok.ru/video/3716271639269
```

Bad:

```
https://ok6-14.vkuser.net/expires/1787847138608/...
```

The second one expires.

---

# Fix 4: Your frame extraction has the same issue

Here:

```python
stream_target = self._resolve_direct_stream_url(stream_url)
```

If:

```python
stream_url = vkuser.net
```

you return immediately:

```python
if self._is_direct_stream(url_or_path):
    return url_or_path
```

Then ffmpeg gets a dead CDN URL.

Better:

```python
def _resolve_direct_stream_url(self,url_or_path):

    if not url_or_path.startswith("http"):
        return url_or_path

    # only trust manifests
    if ".m3u8" in url_or_path or ".mpd" in url_or_path:
        return url_or_path

    meta = self.probe_metadata(url_or_path)

    return meta.stream_path
```

---

# The OpenCV warning

This:

```
cv2.VideoCapture.open VIDEOIO(CV_IMAGES)
expected '0?[1-9][du]' pattern
```

is a symptom.

OpenCV thinks:

```
https://....
```

is an image sequence.

Because it cannot open the stream.

Do not debug OpenCV yet.

Fix ingestion first.

```python
from src.ingestion import StreamIngestionService

s = StreamIngestionService()
```

| frame extraction        | ⚠️ same root problem           |

Your architecture is actually close. The main mistake is treating **resolved CDN video URLs as universal streams**. Keep the original platform URL and let `yt-dlp` resolve the correct media format.

145. The logs reveal a **chain reaction failure**. The job isn't just "stuck"; it's failing sequentially because the audio extraction is producing an invalid file, which then causes Whisper to fail, which then causes the VLM fallback to crash.

Here is the breakdown of what is happening in your logs:

### 1. The Root Cause: Invalid Audio Extraction
```text
INFO:src.ingestion:Selected format: id=mp4 ext=mp4 acodec=None vcodec=None
```
*   **What this means:** `yt_dlp` found a video-only stream (no audio track). Your code selected it anyway because there were no better options.
*   **The Consequence:** When FFmpeg tries to extract audio from a video-only stream, it produces either an **empty WAV file** or a silent/corrupt one.
*   **Why it didn't crash immediately:** Your `extract_audio_stream` method checks `out_path.stat().st_size > 0`. An empty FFmpeg output might still have a header (44 bytes), passing the size check but containing no actual audio data.

### 2. The First Domino: Whisper Fails
```text
INFO:groq._base_client:Retrying request to /openai/v1/audio/transcriptions...
WARNING:src.pipeline:Tier 1 failed: Connection error.
```
*   **What happened:** You sent the empty/corrupt WAV file to Groq/Whisper.
*   **Why it failed:** The API likely rejected the file as invalid audio or timed out trying to process silence. The "Connection error" is often a symptom of the API hanging on bad input before eventually dropping the connection.

### 3. The Second Domino: VLM Fallback Crashes
```text
ERROR:src.fallback_vlm:VLM candidate arbitration failed: ... 'At most 1 image(s) may be provided in one prompt. None'
```
*   **What happened:** Because Tier 1 (Whisper) failed, your pipeline tried to fall back to the Vision Language Model (VLM).
*   **Why it crashed:** The VLM expects an image path. Since the audio extraction failed silently, no frame was captured, so `frame_image_path` was `None`. The NVIDIA API received `null` instead of an image URL and returned a `400 Bad Request`.

---

### How to Fix This Chain Reaction

You need to add **defensive checks** at every stage so one failure doesn't corrupt the next.

#### Fix 1: Validate Audio Before Sending to Whisper
In your pipeline, check if the WAV file is actually valid before calling STT:

```python
# In your pipeline.py, before calling Whisper:
import wave

def is_valid_wav(path: Path) -> bool:
    try:
        with wave.open(str(path), 'rb') as wf:
            # Check if it has frames and reasonable duration
            frames = wf.getnframes()
            rate = wf.getframerate()
            duration = frames / rate if rate > 0 else 0
            return duration > 1.0  # Reject files shorter than 1 second
    except Exception:
        return False

if not is_valid_wav(audio_path):
    raise IngestionError(f"Extracted audio file is invalid or empty: {audio_path}")
```

#### Fix 2: Handle Video-Only Streams Properly
In `extract_audio_stream`, when `yt_dlp` returns a format with `acodec=None`, you should either:
*   Skip it and look for a muxed stream (video+audio together)
*   Or force a full download (`allow_download=True`) since streaming audio-only won't work

Update your format selection logic:
```python
# Prefer formats that actually have audio
audio_formats = [
    f for f in formats
    if isinstance(f, dict) 
    and f.get("url")
    and (f.get("acodec") not in (None, "none") or f.get("vcodec") == "none")
]

# If no pure audio formats, look for muxed streams
if not audio_formats:
    audio_formats = [
        f for f in formats
        if isinstance(f, dict) 
        and f.get("url") 
        and f.get("acodec") not in (None, "none")  # Must have audio
    ]
```

#### Fix 3: Guard the VLM Fallback
In `fallback_vlm.py`, validate inputs before calling the API:

```python
if not frame_image_path or not Path(frame_image_path).exists():
    logger.error("VLM fallback skipped: No valid frame image available")
    return None  # Or raise a specific error
    
# Also validate the prompt/images list before sending
if not images or len(images) == 0:
    raise ValueError("VLM requires at least one image")
```

### Immediate Action
Your current job `job_39bc51be5f` is dead. Submit a new job, but first apply **Fix 2** to ensure you're not selecting video-only streams. The `acodec=None` log line is the smoking gun here.

146. This is a massive improvement. You have successfully broken the chain reaction of failures by adding validation at every stage.

However, looking closely at your updated `src/ingestion.py`, there is **one critical logic gap** remaining that will cause the exact same "silent failure" you saw in the logs (`Selected format: id=mp4 ext=mp4 acodec=None`).

### The Remaining Bug: Fallback to Video-Only Streams

In `extract_audio_stream`, look at this block:

```python
# ... filtering for audio_formats ...
if audio_formats:
    # ... select best audio format ...
elif isinstance(info, dict) and info.get("url"):
    logger.warning("No strict audio format found; falling back to default URL.")
    audio_url = info.get("url")  # <--- DANGER ZONE
```

**Why this is dangerous:**
When OK.ru provides separate streams (DASH-like behavior), `info.get("url")` often points to the **video-only** manifest or stream because yt_dlp defaults to the highest quality video when no single muxed stream exists. By assigning this to `audio_url`, you are telling FFmpeg to extract audio from a file that has *no audio track*. FFmpeg will succeed (return code 0) but produce an empty/silent WAV file, which then passes your `is_valid_wav` check if it has a header, or fails Whisper silently.

### The Fix: Force Muxed Download When No Audio Stream Exists

Replace that `elif` block with a hard fail that triggers your existing `allow_download` safety net:

```python
                    if audio_formats:
                        def format_score(f: dict) -> Tuple[int, int, int, float]:
                            u = f.get("url", "")
                            p = f.get("protocol", "")
                            vc = f.get("vcodec", "")
                            is_audio_only = 1 if vc == "none" else 0
                            is_hls = 1 if (p.startswith("m3u8") or ".m3u8" in u or u.endswith("/video/")) else 0
                            is_direct_http = 1 if (p in ("http", "https") and not is_hls) else 0
                            height = f.get("height") or 0
                            abr = f.get("abr") or 0.0
                            return (is_audio_only, is_direct_http, -height, abr)

                        audio_formats.sort(key=format_score, reverse=True)
                        selected = audio_formats[0]
                        audio_url = selected["url"]

                        logger.info(
                            "Selected format: id=%s ext=%s acodec=%s vcodec=%s",
                            selected.get("format_id"),
                            selected.get("ext"),
                            selected.get("acodec"),
                            selected.get("vcodec"),
                        )
                    # REMOVED THE DANGEROUS elif BLOCK ENTIRELY
                    # If we reach here without audio_url, we MUST fall back to full download
```

Then, right before the FFmpeg execution block, add this guard:

```python
        if not audio_url:
            # CRITICAL: Do NOT attempt streaming extraction without a verified audio URL
            # This forces the allow_download fallback below to handle muxed streams properly
            logger.warning("No valid audio stream URL available; skipping direct extraction.")
        elif audio_url:
            ffmpeg_cmd = [
                "ffmpeg",
                "-y",
                "-threads", "1",
                "-user_agent", user_agent,
                "-referer", referer,
                "-reconnect", "1",
                "-reconnect_streamed", "1",
                "-reconnect_delay_max", "5",
                "-i", audio_url,
                "-vn",
                "-ac", "1",
                "-ar", "16000",
                "-acodec", "pcm_s16le",
                str(out_path),
            ]
            # ... rest of FFmpeg logic unchanged ...
```

### Why This Works With Your Existing Code

Your `extract_audio_stream` already has a perfect safety net at the bottom:

```python
        if allow_download:
            logger.warning("Direct audio streaming failed; falling back to full download...")
            try:
                cached_video = self.download_video_cache(url_or_path, job_id or "default", target_dir)
                # ... extracts audio from local MP4 ...
```

By removing the dangerous fallback to `info.get("url")`, when no audio-only stream is found, `audio_url` stays `None`. The FFmpeg block is skipped entirely, and if `allow_download=True` (which you should set in production pipelines for OK.ru), it cleanly falls back to downloading the full muxed video and extracting audio locally — which **always works** because the file physically contains both tracks.

### Pipeline Recommendation

In your `DetectionPipeline.run_job`, change:

```python
audio_path = self.ingestion_service.extract_audio_stream(
    video_url, job_id=job_id, allow_download=False  # <-- CHANGE THIS
)
```

to:

```python
audio_path = self.ingestion_service.extract_audio_stream(
    video_url, job_id=job_id, allow_download=True   # <-- ENABLE FALLBACK
)
```

OK.ru's CDN architecture frequently separates audio/video streams. For any platform that uses adaptive streaming, `allow_download=True` should be the default, not the exception. The streaming path is an optimization for platforms like YouTube that provide muxed formats; for OK.ru, it's inherently unreliable.

With these two changes (removing the dangerous fallback + enabling `allow_download=True`), your defensive checks in `pipeline.py` and `fallback_vlm.py` will actually protect against real failures instead of silently passing through corrupted data.

147. import json
import logging
import math
import shutil
import tempfile
import wave
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, List, Optional, Union

import cv2

from src.config import settings
from src.fallback_vlm import VLMArbiterService, VLMError
from src.ingestion import IngestionError, StreamIngestionService
from src.models.schemas import (
    CandidateFrame,
    DetectionResult,
    JobRequest,
    JobStatus,
    STTResult,
    TierType,
)
from src.primary_ocr import (
    OCRError,
    scan_dense_window,
    scan_sparse_timeline,
)
from src.primary_stt import SpeechToTextService

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def format_timestamp(seconds: float) -> str:
    if not math.isfinite(seconds) or seconds < 0:
        seconds = 0.0

    hrs = int(seconds // 3600)
    mins = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)
    secs = seconds % 60

    return f"{hrs:02d}:{mins:02d}:{secs:06.3f}"


def calculate_confidence_score(
    text_similarity: float,
    ocr_confidence: float = 0.0,
    alignment_score: float = 0.0,
    w_sim: float = 0.5,
    w_ocr: float = 0.3,
    w_align: float = 0.2,
) -> float:
    score = (
        w_sim * text_similarity
        + w_ocr * ocr_confidence
        + w_align * alignment_score
    )
    return round(max(0.0, min(1.0, score)), 4)


def _sanitize_path(val: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    if isinstance(val, (str, Path)) and not hasattr(val, "_mock_return_value"):
        value = str(val).strip()
        return value if value else None
    return None


def is_valid_wav(path: Union[str, Path]) -> bool:
    """Validates that extracted audio is a non-empty WAV with duration > 1.0s."""
    try:
        p = Path(path)
        if not p.exists() or p.stat().st_size < 44:
            return False
        with wave.open(str(p), "rb") as wf:
            frames = wf.getnframes()
            rate = wf.getframerate()
            duration = frames / rate if rate > 0 else 0
            return duration > 1.0
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("WAV validation check failed for %s: %s", path, exc)
        return False


class PipelineOrchestrator:
    def __init__(
        self,
        ingestion_service: Optional[StreamIngestionService] = None,
        stt_service: Optional[SpeechToTextService] = None,
        vlm_service: Optional[VLMArbiterService] = None,
        mistral_client: Optional[Any] = None,
    ):
        self.ingestion_service = ingestion_service or StreamIngestionService()
        self.stt_service = stt_service or SpeechToTextService()
        self.vlm_service = vlm_service or VLMArbiterService()
        self.mistral_client = mistral_client

    def _extract_single_frame(
        self,
        video_source: str,
        timestamp_seconds: float,
        fps: float,
        dest_path: Path,
    ) -> bool:
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_source)

        if not cap.isOpened():
            logger.warning(
                "Unable to open stream: %s", str(video_source or "")[:100]
            )
            return False

        try:
            frame_number = int(round(timestamp_seconds * fps))
            cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, frame_number)

            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if not ret or frame is None:
                return False

            dest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return bool(cv2.imwrite(str(dest_path), frame))
        finally:
            cap.release()

    def _persist_frame(
        self,
        artifacts_dir: Path,
        job_id: str,
        tier_label: str,
        video_source: Optional[str],
        timestamp_seconds: float,
        fps: float,
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        if not video_source:
            return None

        artifacts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        frame_path = artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_{tier_label}.jpg"

        if self.ingestion_service.extract_frame_on_demand(
            video_source, timestamp_seconds, frame_path
        ) and frame_path.exists():
            logger.info("Frame extracted from stream at %.2fs", timestamp_seconds)
            return str(frame_path)

        if self._extract_single_frame(
            video_source, timestamp_seconds, fps, frame_path
        ):
            return str(frame_path)

        logger.warning("Unable to extract frame at %.2fs", timestamp_seconds)
        return None

    def _save_job_metadata(
        self, artifacts_dir: Path, result: DetectionResult
    ) -> None:
        try:
            artifacts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            metadata_file = artifacts_dir / "metadata.json"
            metadata_file.write_text(
                json.dumps(result.model_dump(), indent=2)
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Metadata save failed: %s", e)

    def run(self, job_id: str, request: JobRequest) -> DetectionResult:
        temp_dir_str = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"quest1_{job_id}_")
        temp_dir = Path(temp_dir_str)

        artifacts_base = getattr(
            settings,
            "artifacts_dir",
            getattr(settings, "artifact_storage_dir", Path("artifacts")),
        )
        artifacts_dir = Path(artifacts_base) / job_id

        logger.info("Executing job %s", job_id)

        try:
            metadata = self.ingestion_service.probe_metadata(request.url)
            fps = metadata.fps if metadata.fps > 0 else 25.0
            video_source = metadata.stream_path or request.url

            if metadata.is_local:
                logger.info("Processing local video: %s", video_source)
            else:
                logger.info(
                    "Processing remote stream: %s", str(video_source or "")[:120]
                )

            # --- TIER 0 : SUBTITLE MATCH ---
            if metadata.has_subtitles:
                tier0_result = (
                    self.ingestion_service.probe_embedded_subtitles_match(
                        request.url,
                        request.target_text,
                        similarity_threshold=85.0,
                    )
                )

                if (
                    tier0_result
                    and tier0_result.similarity_score >= 0.85
                ):
                    timestamp = tier0_result.start_time
                    frame = self._persist_frame(
                        artifacts_dir,
                        job_id,
                        "tier0",
                        video_source,
                        timestamp,
                        fps,
                    )

                    result = DetectionResult(
                        job_id=job_id,
                        status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                        target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                        timestamp_seconds=timestamp,
                        formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(timestamp),
                        frame_number=int(timestamp * fps),
                        extracted_text=tier0_result.matched_text,
                        confidence_score=tier0_result.similarity_score,
                        tier_executed=TierType.TIER_0_SUBTITLE,
                        frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(frame),
                    )

                    self._save_job_metadata(artifacts_dir, result)
                    return result

            # --- TIER 1 : STREAM AUDIO -> WHISPER STT ---
            stt_result = STTResult(found=False)

            try:
                logger.info("Extracting audio directly from stream")
                audio_path = self.ingestion_service.extract_audio_stream(
                    video_source,
                    job_id=job_id,
                    output_dir=temp_dir,
                    allow_download=True,  # Guarded adaptive stream fallback
                )

                if isinstance(audio_path, str):
                    audio_path = Path(audio_path)

                if is_valid_wav(audio_path):
                    logger.info("Running Whisper STT on valid audio")
                    words = self.stt_service.transcribe_audio(audio_path)
                    stt_result = self.stt_service.align_target_dialogue(
                        words, request.target_text
                    )
                else:
                    logger.warning("Extracted WAV audio is invalid or silent. Skipping STT alignment.")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning("Tier 1 failed: %s", e)

            if stt_result.found and stt_result.confidence >= 0.70:
                logger.info("STT match found at %.2fs", stt_result.start_time)

                # --- TIER 3 : Dense OCR confirmation ---
                start_time = max(0.0, stt_result.start_time - 1.5)
                end_time = min(
                    metadata.duration_seconds, stt_result.end_time + 1.5
                )

                if self.mistral_client:
                    try:
                        dense_frame = artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_tier3.jpg"
                        candidate = scan_dense_window(
                            video_source,
                            start_time,
                            end_time,
                            fps,
                            request.target_text,
                            self.mistral_client,
                            save_frame_path=dense_frame,
                        )

                        if candidate:
                            confidence = calculate_confidence_score(
                                stt_result.confidence,
                                getattr(candidate, "ocr_confidence", 0.8),
                                1.0,
                            )

                            result = DetectionResult(
                                job_id=job_id,
                                status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                                timestamp_seconds=candidate.timestamp_seconds,
                                formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(
                                    candidate.timestamp_seconds
                                ),
                                frame_number=candidate.frame_number,
                                extracted_text=candidate.ocr_detected_text,
                                confidence_score=confidence,
                                tier_executed=TierType.TIER_3_DENSE_OCR,
                                frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(
                                    candidate.image_path
                                ),
                            )

                            self._save_job_metadata(artifacts_dir, result)
                            return result
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning("Dense OCR confirmation failed: %s", e)

                # STT ONLY fallback
                frame = self._persist_frame(
                    artifacts_dir,
                    job_id,
                    "tier1",
                    video_source,
                    stt_result.start_time,
                    fps,
                )

                result = DetectionResult(
                    job_id=job_id,
                    status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                    target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                    timestamp_seconds=stt_result.start_time,
                    formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(
                        stt_result.start_time
                    ),
                    frame_number=int(stt_result.start_time * fps),
                    extracted_text=stt_result.matched_text,
                    confidence_score=stt_result.confidence,
                    tier_executed=TierType.TIER_1_STT,
                    frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(frame),
                )

                self._save_job_metadata(artifacts_dir, result)
                return result

            # --- TIER 2 : SPARSE OCR STREAM SCAN ---
            sparse_candidate = None

            if self.mistral_client:
                try:
                    sparse_frame = artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_tier2.jpg"
                    sparse_candidate = scan_sparse_timeline(
                        video_source,
                        request.target_text,
                        self.mistral_client,
                        sample_fps=getattr(settings, "sparse_ocr_fps", 0.5),
                        save_frame_path=sparse_frame,
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning("Sparse OCR failed: %s", e)

            if sparse_candidate:
                confidence = getattr(sparse_candidate, "ocr_confidence", 0.0)

                if confidence >= 0.60:
                    start = max(0.0, sparse_candidate.timestamp_seconds - 2.0)
                    end = min(
                        metadata.duration_seconds,
                        sparse_candidate.timestamp_seconds + 2.0,
                    )

                    # --- TIER 3 FOLLOW-UP ---
                    try:
                        dense = scan_dense_window(
                            video_source,
                            start,
                            end,
                            fps,
                            request.target_text,
                            self.mistral_client,
                            save_frame_path=(
                                artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_tier3.jpg"
                            ),
                        )

                        if dense:
                            result = DetectionResult(
                                job_id=job_id,
                                status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                                timestamp_seconds=dense.timestamp_seconds,
                                formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(
                                    dense.timestamp_seconds
                                ),
                                frame_number=dense.frame_number,
                                extracted_text=dense.ocr_detected_text,
                                confidence_score=dense.ocr_confidence,
                                tier_executed=TierType.TIER_3_DENSE_OCR,
                                frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(
                                    dense.image_path
                                ),
                            )

                            self._save_job_metadata(artifacts_dir, result)
                            return result
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning("Tier3 after sparse failed: %s", e)

            # --- TIER 4 : VLM FALLBACK ---
            candidates = self._extract_real_vlm_candidates(
                temp_dir,
                video_source,
                stt_result,
                metadata,
            )

            if candidates:
                decision = self.vlm_service.evaluate_candidates(
                    request.target_text, candidates
                )

                if decision.selected_candidate_id != "NONE":
                    selected = next(
                        c
                        for c in candidates
                        if c.candidate_id == decision.selected_candidate_id
                    )

                    artifacts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    persistent_frame_path = (
                        artifacts_dir / f"{job_id}_{selected.candidate_id}.jpg"
                    )
                    try:
                        shutil.copy2(selected.image_path, persistent_frame_path)
                        final_vlm_path: Optional[str] = str(persistent_frame_path)
                    except Exception as copy_err:
                        logger.warning(
                            "Failed persisting VLM candidate frame artifact: %s", copy_err
                        )
                        final_vlm_path = selected.image_path

                    result = DetectionResult(
                        job_id=job_id,
                        status=JobStatus.COMPLETED,
                        target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                        timestamp_seconds=selected.timestamp_seconds,
                        formatted_timestamp=format_timestamp(
                            selected.timestamp_seconds
                        ),
                        frame_number=selected.frame_number,
                        extracted_text=decision.exact_detected_text,
                        confidence_score=decision.confidence_score,
                        tier_executed=TierType.TIER_4_VLM_FALLBACK,
                        frame_image_path=_sanitize_path(final_vlm_path),
                    )

                    self._save_job_metadata(artifacts_dir, result)
                    return result

            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=(
                    "Target dialogue not found through any execution tier."
                ),
            )

        except IngestionError as e:
            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=f"Ingestion failed: {e}",
            )
        except VLMError as e:
            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=f"VLM failed: {e}",
            )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception("Pipeline crashed: %s", e)
            return DetectionResult(
                job_id=job_id,
                status=JobStatus.FAILED,
                target_dialogue=request.target_text,
                error_message=str(e),
            )
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(temp_dir_str, ignore_errors=True)

    def _extract_real_vlm_candidates(
        self, temp_dir: Path, video_source: str, stt_result: STTResult, metadata: Any
    ) -> List[CandidateFrame]:
        candidates = []
        fps = metadata.fps if metadata.fps > 0 else 25.0
        duration = metadata.duration_seconds if metadata.duration_seconds > 0 else 10.0

        if stt_result and stt_result.found and stt_result.start_time is not None:
            base = stt_result.start_time
            timestamps = [
                max(0.0, base - 3.0),
                max(0.0, base - 2.0),
                max(0.0, base - 1.0),
                base,
                min(duration, base + 1.0),
                min(duration, base + 2.0),
                min(duration, base + 3.0),
            ]
        else:
            timestamps = [duration * (i / 8.0) for i in range(1, 8)]

        for idx, ts in enumerate(timestamps, start=1):
            frame_path = temp_dir / f"candidate_C{idx}.jpg"

            self.ingestion_service.extract_frame_on_demand(
                video_source, ts, frame_path
            )

            if not frame_path.exists():
                cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_source)
                if cap.isOpened():
                    frame_num = int(round(ts * fps))
                    cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, float(frame_num))
                    ret, frame = cap.read()
                    if ret and frame is not None:
                        cv2.imwrite(str(frame_path), frame)
                    cap.release()

            if frame_path.exists():
                candidates.append(
                    CandidateFrame(
                        candidate_id=f"C{idx}",
                        timestamp_seconds=round(ts, 3),
                        frame_number=int(round(ts * fps)),
                        image_path=str(frame_path),
                    )
                )

        return candidates
